<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Matthew buys $2$ sandwiches and a cookie with a $\pounds 5$ note.

How much change does he receive?

| Price List |                  |
|:----------:|:---------------:|
|  Sandwich  | $\pounds 1.49 $ |
|   Cookie   | $\pounds 0.69 $ |
|  Ice Lolly | $\pounds 0.49 $ |
|    Soda    | $\pounds 0.99 $ |


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Matthew buys &#36;2&#36; sandwiches and a cookie with a &#36;\pounds 5&#36; note.

How much change does he receive?

| Price List |                  |
|:----------:|:---------------:|
|  Sandwich  | &#36;\pounds 1.49 &#36; |
|   Cookie   | &#36;\pounds 0.69 &#36; |
|  Ice Lolly | &#36;\pounds 0.49 &#36; |
|    Soda    | &#36;\pounds 0.99 &#36; |

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

From the table,


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 2 \times 1.49 + 0.69 \\\\
                        &= 2.98 + 0.69 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3.67 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 3.67 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.33
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.33$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which is better value, $600 \ \text{g}$ for $\pounds 3$ or $500 \ \text{g}$ for $\pounds 2.75?$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which is better value, &#36;600 \ \text{g}&#36; for &#36;\pounds 3&#36; or &#36;500 \ \text{g}&#36; for &#36;\pounds 2.75?&#36; 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's calculate the cost per gram for both.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {First}           &= \dfrac {\pounds 3} {600} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {300 \ \text{p}} {600} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {300} {300 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{300}} {\cancel{300} \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                        &= 0.5 \ \text{p per gram} \\\\
\text {Second}          &= \dfrac {\pounds 2.75} {500} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {275 \ \text{p}} {500} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 55} {5 \times 100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 55} {\cancel {5} \times 100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {55} {100} \\\\
                        &= 0.55 \ \text{p per gram}
\end{aligned}
$

Better value $= 600 \ \text {g}$ for $\pounds 3$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$600 \ \text{g} \ \text{for} \ \pounds 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let's calculate the cost per gram for both.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {First}           &= \dfrac {\pounds 3} {600} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {300 \ \text{p}} {600} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {300} {300 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{300}} {\cancel{300} \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                        &= 0.5 \ \text{p per gram} \\\\
\text {Second}          &= \dfrac {\pounds 2.75} {500} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {275 \ \text{p}} {500} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 55} {5 \times 100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 55} {\cancel {5} \times 100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {55} {100} \\\\
                        &= 0.55 \ \text{p per gram}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

Better value &#36;= 600 \ \text {g}&#36; for &#36;\pounds 3&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The local bakery sells muffins at $85 \ \text{p}$ each. I have $\pounds 15$ to spend. How many muffins can I buy? 

- $17$
- $16$
- $15$
- $14$
- $20$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Count of muffins}    &= \dfrac {\text {Total money}} {\text {Cost of one muffin}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\pounds 15} {85 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1500 \ \text{p}} {85 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 300} {5 \times 17} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 300} {\cancel {5} \times 17} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {300} {17} \\\\
                            &= 17 \ \text {remainder} \ 11 \\\\
                            &\approx 17
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the difference between $10\%$ of $\pounds 20$ and $20\%$ of $\pounds 10$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the difference between &#36;10\%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 20&#36; and &#36;20\%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 10&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 20 - 20\% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 10 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 20 - \dfrac {20} {100} \times 10 \\\\     
&= \dfrac {10 \times 20} {100} - \dfrac {20 \times 10} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel {0} \times 2 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0} } - \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times 1 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= 2-2 \\\\
&= 0
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$0$

</div>
</div>

|             Sausage            | $\pounds \ 0.45 $ |
|           Cup of Tea           | $\pounds \ 0.55 $ |

| Can of Day-Glo Sparkling Drink | $\pounds \ 0.35 $ |

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I have $\pounds 8.17$ credit on my mobile phone. At the end of a phone call this falls 
to $\pounds 7.65$. 
How much did the call cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I have &#36;\pounds 8.17&#36; credit on my mobile phone. At the end of a phone call this falls 
to &#36;\pounds 7.65&#36;. 
How much did the call cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of the call}     &= 8.17 - 7.65 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 0.52
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 0.52$

</div>
</div>

<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
tmoney
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Fiona buys:

* $3$ cans of coke $@ \ 85 \ \text{p}$ per can
* $5$ samosas $@ \ 30 \ \text{p}$ per samosa

If Fiona pays with a $\pounds 5$ note, how much change does she get?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Fiona buys:

* &#36;3&#36; cans of coke &#36;@ \ 85 \ \text{p}&#36; per can
* &#36;5&#36; samosas &#36;@ \ 30 \ \text{p}&#36; per samosa

If Fiona pays with a &#36;\pounds 5&#36; note, how much change does she get?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 3 \times 85 + 5 \times 30 \\\\
                        &= 255 + 150 \\\\
                        &= 405 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.05 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 4.05 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 0.95
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 0.95$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 3 \times 85 + 5 \times 30 \\\\
                        &= 255 + 150 \\\\
                        &= 405 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.05 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 4.05 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 0.95
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\pounds 0.95&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the difference in price between a small and large beer. Give your answer in shillings and pence.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the difference in price between a small and large beer. Give your answer in shillings and pence.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}  &= (1 \ \text{pound} \ 4 \ \text {shillings} \ 9 \ \text{p}) - (14 \ \text {shillings} \ 5 \ \text{p}) \\\\
                    &= (20 \ \text {shillings} + 4 \ \text {shillings} + 9 \ \text{p}) - (14 \ \text {shillings} + 5 \ \text{p}) \\\\
                    &= (24 \ \text {shillings} + 9 \ \text{p}) - (14 \ \text {shillings} + 5 \ \text{p}) \\\\
                    &= 24 \ \text {shillings} + 9 \ \text{p} - 14 \ \text {shillings} - 5 \ \text{p} \\\\
                    &= 10 \ \text {shillings} \ 4 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10 \ \text {shillings} \ 4 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the total cost of one large and two small beers. Give your answer in pounds, shillings and pence.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the total cost of one large and two small beers. Give your answer in pounds, shillings and pence.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= (1 \ \text{pound} \ 4 \ \text {shillings} \ 9 \ \text{p}) + 2 \times (14 \ \text {shillings} \ 5 \ \text{p}) \\\\
                    &= 1 \ \text{pound} + 4 \ \text {shillings} + 9 \ \text{p} + 28 \ \text {shillings} + 10 \ \text{p} \\\\
                    &= 1 \ \text{pound} + 32 \ \text {shillings} + 19 \ \text{p} \\\\
                    &= 1 \ \text{pound} + (1 \ \text{pound} + 12 \ \text {shillings}) + (1 \ \text {shillings} + 7 \ \text{p}) \\\\
                    &= 1 \ \text{pound} + 1 \ \text{pound} + 12 \ \text {shillings} + 1 \ \text {shillings} + 7 \ \text{p} \\\\
                    &= 2 \ \text{pound} \ 13 \ \text {shillings} \ 7 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \ \text{pound} \ 13 \ \text {shillings} \ 7 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

                    &= 1 \ \text{pound} + 1 \ \text{pound} + 12 \ \text {shillings} + 1 \ \text {shillings} + 7 \ \text{p} \\\\

</code></pre>

</span>

<div class="question subsubquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Caley buys $3$ identical ice lollies and pays $\pounds 1.92$. 

What is the cost, in pence, of one ice lolly?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Caley buys &#36;3&#36; identical ice lollies and pays &#36;\pounds 1.92&#36;. 

What is the cost, in pence, of one ice lolly?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of one ice lolly}   &= \dfrac {\pounds 1.92} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {192 \ \text{p}} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 64} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 64} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                                &= 64 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the cost of one grapefruit?

Give your answer in pence.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

                             &= \dfrac {329 \ \text {p}} {7}  \\\\

                             &= 47 \ \text {p}
\end{aligned}
$

How much change should he receive?

<div class="papername">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
tmoney
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Suki buys $500 \ \text{g}$ of sugar at $\pounds 1.10$ per kilogram and $750 \ \text{g}$ of plain flour at $\pounds 1.12$ per kilogram. 
How much change did she receive from a $\pounds 5$ note?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Suki buys &#36;500 \ \text{g}&#36; of sugar at &#36;\pounds 1.10&#36; per kilogram and &#36;750 \ \text{g}&#36; of plain flour at &#36;\pounds 1.12&#36; per kilogram. 
How much change did she receive from a &#36;\pounds 5&#36; note?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of} \ 500 \ \text {g sugar}     &= \dfrac {500} {1000} \ \text{kg} \times 1.10 \\\\
                                            &= 0.5 \times 1.10 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 0.55 \\\\
\text {Cost of} \ 750 \ \text {g flour}     &= \dfrac {750} {1000} \ \text{kg} \times 1.12 \\\\
                                            &= 0.75 \times 1.12 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 0.84 \\\\
\text {Total cost}                          &= 0.55 + 0.84 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 1.39 \\\\
\text {Change received}                     &= 5 - 1.39 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 3.61
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 3.61$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>

                                            &= 0.5 \times 1.10 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 0.55 \\\\
\text {Cost of} \ 750 \ \text {g flour}     &= \dfrac {750} {1000} \ \text{kg} \times 1.12 \\\\
                                            &= 0.75 \times 1.12 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 0.84 \\\\
\text {Total cost}                          &= 0.55 + 0.84 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 1.39 \\\\
\text {Change received}                     &= 5 - 1.39 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 3.61
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\pounds 3.61&#36;

<div class="uuid">
<p>D2BE2238-60CC-4D8A-94E4-E86779D1F0CC</p>
</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2008--group-2:1:18</p>

<div class="rag">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
tmoney
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Charlotte buys $500 \ \text{g}$ of cheese which costs $\pounds 5.30$ per kilogram, six bananas at $15 \ \text{p}$ each and two chocolate bars at $56 \ \text{p}$ each. How much change will she have from a ten pound note?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Charlotte buys &#36;500 \ \text{g}&#36; of cheese which costs &#36;\pounds 5.30&#36; per kilogram, six bananas at &#36;15 \ \text{p}&#36; each and two chocolate bars at &#36;56 \ \text{p}&#36; each. How much change will she have from a ten pound note?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cheese}           &= \dfrac {500} {1000} \ \text{kg} \times 5.30 \\\\
                        &= 0.5 \times 5.30 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.65 \\\\
\text{Bananas}          &= 6 \times 15 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 6 \times \pounds \dfrac {15} {100} \\\\
                        &= 6 \times 0.15 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 0.9 \\\\
\text{Chocolate bars}   &= 2 \times 56 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 2 \times \pounds \dfrac {56} {100} \\\\
                        &= 2 \times 0.56 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.12 \\\\
\text {Total cost}      &= 2.65 + 0.9 + 1.12 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.67 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 4.67 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.33
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.33$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cheese}           &= \dfrac {500} {1000} \ \text{kg} \times 5.30 \\\\
                        &= 0.5 \times 5.30 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.65 \\\\
\text{Bananas}          &= 6 \times 15 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 6 \times \pounds \dfrac {15} {100} \\\\
                        &= 6 \times 0.15 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 0.9 \\\\
\text{Chocolate bars}   &= 2 \times 56 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 2 \times \pounds \dfrac {56} {100} \\\\
                        &= 2 \times 0.56 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.12 \\\\
\text {Total cost}      &= 2.65 + 0.9 + 1.12 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.67 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 4.67 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.33
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>

</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\pounds 5.33&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**


Maria telephoned Priya on her mobile. The rate for the first three minutes was $7 \ \text{p}$ per minute. After that the telephone call cost $5 \ \text{p}$ per minute. The telephone call lasted $21 \ \text{minutes}$. How much did it cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">
Maria telephoned Priya on her mobile. The rate for the first three minutes was &#36;7 \ \text{p}&#36; per minute. After that the telephone call cost &#36;5 \ \text{p}&#36; per minute. The telephone call lasted &#36;21 \ \text{minutes}&#36;. How much did it cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total cost}   &= 7 \times 3 + 5 \times (21 -3) \\\\
                    &= 21 + 5 \times 18 \\\\
                    &= 21 + 90 \\\\
                    &= 111 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$111 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Miriam pays for two of these magazines with a &#36;\pounds 10&#36; note. How much change should she receive?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= 2 \times 2.28 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.56 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 4.56 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.44 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.44$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$32$ students and $4$ members of staff from Encrypt School are going to a lecture on code-breaking.

<img src="/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2009/group-1/questions/s01-q15-i00.png" alt="papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2009/group-1/questions/s01-q15-i00.png" title="papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2009/group-1/questions/s01-q15-i00.png" data-slot="paper-image" data-kind="essential" data-size="normal" data-align="center" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 32rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />

Code-Breaking Lecture

Ticket Prices

Students: $\pounds 14.00$ each
Adults: $\pounds 15.00$ each

$1$ free adult ticket provided with every $10$ student tickets purchased

How much will the tickets cost in total for the school party?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;32&#36; students and &#36;4&#36; members of staff from Encrypt School are going to a lecture on code-breaking.

&lt;PaperImage kind="essential" /&gt;

Code-Breaking Lecture

Ticket Prices

Students: &#36;\pounds 14.00&#36; each
Adults: &#36;\pounds 15.00&#36; each

&#36;1&#36; free adult ticket provided with every &#36;10&#36; student tickets purchased

How much will the tickets cost in total for the school party?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Free adult tickets}  &= \dfrac{32}{10} \\\\
                            &= 3 \ \text{remainder} \ 2 \\\\
                            &\approx 3
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= \text{Students cost} + \text{Adults cost} \\\\
                    &= 32 \times 14 + (4 - 3) \times 15 \\\\
                    &= 32 \times 14 + 1 \times 15 \\\\
                    &= 448 + 15 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 463
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 463$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

\text {Free adult tickets}  &= \dfrac{32}{10} \\\\
                            &= 3 \ \text{remainder} \ 2 \\\\
                            &\approx 3
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= \text{Students cost} + \text{Adults cost} \\\\
                    &= 32 \times 14 + (4 - 3) \times 15 \\\\
                    &= 32 \times 14 + 1 \times 15 \\\\
                    &= 448 + 15 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 463
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alisha wants to exchange the coins in her money box for $\pounds 5$ notes. She has:

Twelve $50 \ \text{p}$ coins

Eighteen $20 \ \text{p}$ coins

Seven $10 \ \text{p}$ coins

Twelve $2 \ \text{p}$ coins

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the greatest number of $\pounds 5$ notes she could receive?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the greatest number of &#36;\pounds 5&#36; notes she could receive?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total amount}   
                                                &= 12 \times 50 \ \text{p} + 18 \times 20 \ \text{p} + 7 \times 10 \ \text{p} + 12 \times 2 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                                &= 600 + 360 + 70 + 24 \\\\
                                                &= 1054 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Number of} \ \pounds 5 \ \text {notes}   &= \dfrac {1054 \ \text{p}} {\pounds 5} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1054 \ \text{p}} {500 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                                &= 2 \ \text {remainder} \ 54 \\\\
                                                &\approx 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much more money does she need to receive another $\pounds 5$ note?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much more money does she need to receive another &#36;\pounds 5&#36; note?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Using the answer from the previous question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money needed}    &= \pounds 5 - 54 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5 - \pounds 0.54 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.46
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 4.46$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The Crystal Cinema shows the following prices for tickets and for popcorn.

**Tickets**

|Adult| $\pounds 6.50$|
|:---:|:-------------:|
|Child |$\pounds 3.00$|

**Popcorn**

|Large |$\pounds 2.20$|
|:----:|:------------:|
|Small |$\pounds 1.50$|

Mr and Mrs Jenkins and their $2$ children go to the cinema. 
Each child has a small bag of popcorn and the adults share a large bag of popcorn.
Mr Jenkins pays for the popcorn and the tickets with a $\pounds 50$ note.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mr Jenkins parks his car at $14{:}30$ and returns to the car at $16{:}25$. 
He paid for $3 \ \text{hours}$ parking.

How much time does he have left on his parking ticket?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

|   Fruits   |      Price     |      Amount     |
|:----------:|:--------------:|:---------------:|
|   bananas  |   $\pounds 2$  |       $3$       |
|  cherries  |  $\pounds 4.5$ | per $\text{kg}$ |
| grapefruit |  $90  \text{p}$ |       $1$       |
|   oranges  | $\pounds 1.60$ |       $5$       |
|  tomatoes  | $\pounds 1.85$ |       $1$       |

She bought $6$ bananas, $2$ grapefruit and half a kilogram of cherries.

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much did her shopping cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much did her shopping cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 6 \times \dfrac{1}{3} \times \pounds 2 + 2 \times 90 \ \text{p} + 0.5 \times \pounds 4.50 \\\\
                    &= 6 \times \dfrac{1}{3} \times \pounds 2 + 2 \times \pounds 0.90 + 0.5 \times \pounds 4.50 \\\\
                    &= 4 + 1.80 + 2.25 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 8.05
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 8.05$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Joanna gets $\pounds 5.00$ pocket money each week and 
her younger sister, Amy, gets $\pounds 3.50$ each week. 
After $5 \ \text{weeks}$, Joanna saved all her money, but Amy 
spent $75 \ \text{p}$ a week on stickers. 

How much more does Joanna now have compared to Amy? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Joanna gets &#36;\pounds 5.00&#36; pocket money each week and 
her younger sister, Amy, gets &#36;\pounds 3.50&#36; each week. 
After &#36;5 \ \text{weeks}&#36;, Joanna saved all her money, but Amy 
spent &#36;75 \ \text{p}&#36; a week on stickers. 

How much more does Joanna now have compared to Amy? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}  &= 5 \times \pounds 5 - (5 \times \pounds 3.50 - 5 \times 75 \ \text{p}) \\\\
                    &= \pounds 25 - (\pounds 17.50 - 5 \times \pounds 0.75) \\\\
                    &= 25 - (17.50 - 3.75) \\\\
                    &= 25 - 13.75 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 11.25
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 11.25$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The largest amount of money that can be made using these coins
 is $38 \ \text{p}$. Find the largest amount of money less 
 than $38 \ \text{p}$ which can not be made using these coins.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The largest amount of money that can be made using these coins
 is &#36;38 \ \text{p}&#36;. Find the largest amount of money less 
 than &#36;38 \ \text{p}&#36; which can not be made using these coins.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let’s list the amounts from $38 \ \text{p}$ downwards, showing which can and cannot be made using the coins.

| Total value     | Coins used                         | 
| :-------------: | :----------------------------------:| 
| $37 \ \text{p}$ | $20 \ \text{p} + 10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$ | 
| $36 \ \text{p}$ | $20 \ \text{p} + 10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$ | 
| $35 \ \text{p}$ | $20 \ \text{p} + 10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p}$ | 
| $34 \ \text{p}$ | Not possible                        | 

Answer $= 34 \ \text{p}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$34 \ \text{p}$

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let’s list the amounts from &#36;38 \ \text{p}&#36; downwards, showing which can and cannot be made using the coins.

| Total value     | Coins used                         | 
| :-------------: | :----------------------------------:| 
| &#36;37 \ \text{p}&#36; | &#36;20 \ \text{p} + 10 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}&#36; |

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Rose buys a book which costs $\pounds 7.46$.

She pays for the book with a $\pounds 10$ note and receives $4$ coins as her change.

Which $4$ coins does Rose receive?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Rose buys a book which costs &#36;\pounds 7.46&#36;.

She pays for the book with a &#36;\pounds 10&#36; note and receives &#36;4&#36; coins as her change.

Which &#36;4&#36; coins does Rose receive?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- The coins available are: $1 \ \text{p}$, $2 \ \text{p}$, $5 \ \text{p}$, $10 \ \text{p}$, $20 \ \text{p}$, $50 \ \text{p}$, $\pounds 1$, $\pounds 2$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Change received}     &= 10 - 7.46 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 2.54 \\\\
\text {Coins recieved}      &= \pounds 2 + 50 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2, \ 50 \ \text{p}, \ 2 \ \text{p}, \ 2 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_coins_available
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>

                            &= \pounds 2.54 \\\\

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the cost of one Doggy Chew?

Give your answer in pence.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the cost of one Doggy Chew?

Give your answer in pence.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of 1 packet}          &= \dfrac {\pounds 3.43} {7} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {343 \ \text {p}} {7}  \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {49 \times 7} {7} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {49 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
                                 &= 49 \ \text {p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$49 \ \text {p}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Paula was given $\pounds 40$ for her birthday.

She used some of this money to buy a scarf for $\pounds 14.95$ and a hat for $\pounds 8.45$

How much of her birthday money does she have left?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Paula was given &#36;\pounds 40&#36; for her birthday.

She used some of this money to buy a scarf for &#36;\pounds 14.95&#36; and a hat for &#36;\pounds 8.45&#36;

How much of her birthday money does she have left?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money  spent}  &= 14.95 + 8.45 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 23.40 \\\\
\text{Money  left}  &= \text{Money given} - \text{Money spent} \\\\
                    &= 40 - 23.40 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 16.60
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 16.60$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sarah has $6$ coins in her pocket, which come to a total of $\pounds 1.75$

Which coins could Sarah have in her pocket?

Give two possible answers.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

Give two possible answers.
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

or

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $15\%$ of $\pounds 3$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out &#36;15\%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 3&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \% \times \pounds 3 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 300 \ \text{p} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 300} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 3 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
&= 15 \times 3 \\\\
&= 45 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$45 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $35 \%$ of $\pounds 4$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out &#36;35 \%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 4&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 35 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \times  4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35 \times 4} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {140} {100} \\\\
&= \pounds 1.40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.40$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $15 \%$ of $\pounds 5$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out &#36;15 \%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 5&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 5 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 500 \ \text{p} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 500} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 5 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 15 \times 5 \\\\
&= 75 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Petrol costs $\pounds 1.12$ per litre. If there are $4.5 \ \text{litres}$ in a gallon, what would the cost of a gallon of petrol be?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Petrol costs &#36;\pounds 1.12&#36; per litre. If there are &#36;4.5 \ \text{litres}&#36; in a gallon, what would the cost of a gallon of petrol be?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of a gallon of petrol}         &= 4.5 \times 1.12 \\\\
                                          &= \pounds 5.04
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.04$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

My garden centre sells Summer bedding plants at $85 \ \text{p}$ each. I have $\pounds 30$ to spend. How many plants can I buy? Show your working.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">My garden centre sells Summer bedding plants at &#36;85 \ \text{p}&#36; each. I have &#36;\pounds 30&#36; to spend. How many plants can I buy? Show your working.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of plants}     &= \dfrac {\text{Total money}} {\text{Cost of a plant}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\pounds 30} {85 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3000 \ \text{p}} {85 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 600} {5 \times 17} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 600} {\cancel {5} \times 17} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {600} {17} \\\\
                            &= 35 \ \text {remainder} \ 5 \\\\
                            &\approx 35
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$35$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of plants}     &= \dfrac {\text{Total money}} {\text{Cost of a plant}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\pounds 30} {85 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3000 \ \text{p}} {85 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 600} {5 \times 17} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 600} {\cancel {5} \times 17} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {600} {17} \\\\
                            &= 35 \ \text {remainder} \ 5 \\\\
                            &\approx 35
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;35&#36;
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

For Ben's birthday he goes to the cinema. Tickets cost $\pounds 3.85$ for children and $\pounds 5.50$ for adults.

In his party there are $4$ children and $2$ adults.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">For Ben's birthday he goes to the cinema. Tickets cost &#36;\pounds 3.85&#36; for children and &#36;\pounds 5.50&#36; for adults.

In his party there are &#36;4&#36; children and &#36;2&#36; adults.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
tmoney
</span>
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Cans of coke cost $\pounds 1.16$ each. I buy $4$ cans and pay with a $\pounds 10$ note. How much change do I receive?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Cans of coke cost &#36;\pounds 1.16&#36; each. I buy &#36;4&#36; cans and pay with a &#36;\pounds 10&#36; note. How much change do I receive?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 1.16 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.64 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 4.64 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.36
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.36$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 1.16 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.64 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 4.64 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.36
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alison went to the supermarket. 

<img src="/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/questions/s01-q18-i00.png" alt="papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/questions/s01-q18-i00.png" title="papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/questions/s01-q18-i00.png" data-slot="paper-image" data-kind="essential" data-size="normal" data-align="center" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 32rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />

She bought two cans of cola costing $53 \ \text{p}$ each, three packets of crisps costing $27 \ \text{p}$ each and a large bar of chocolate that costs $\pounds 1.24$.

How much change did she get from $\pounds 5?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alison went to the supermarket. 

&lt;PaperImage kind="essential" /&gt;

She bought two cans of cola costing &#36;53 \ \text{p}&#36; each, three packets of crisps costing &#36;27 \ \text{p}&#36; each and a large bar of chocolate that costs &#36;\pounds 1.24&#36;.

How much change did she get from &#36;\pounds 5?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 2 \times 53 \ \text{p} + 3 \times 27 \ \text{p} + \pounds 1.24 \\\\
                        &= 2 \times \pounds 0.53 + 3 \times \pounds 0.27 + \pounds 1.24 \\\\
                        &= 1.06 + 0.81 + 1.24 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3.11 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 3.11 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.89
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.89$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 2 \times 53 \ \text{p} + 3 \times 27 \ \text{p} + \pounds 1.24 \\\\
                        &= 2 \times \pounds 0.53 + 3 \times \pounds 0.27 + \pounds 1.24 \\\\
                        &= 1.06 + 0.81 + 1.24 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3.11 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 3.11 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.89
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Danny buys six doughnuts which cost a total of $\pounds 2.76$. How much does each 
doughnut cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Danny buys six doughnuts which cost a total of &#36;\pounds 2.76&#36;. How much does each 
doughnut cost?
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of a doughnut}  &= \dfrac {2.76} {6} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {6 \times 0.46} {6} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel{6} \times 0.46} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 0.46
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Malcolm buys $7$ packets of biscuits at $73 \ \text{p}$ each and $9$ large cakes at $\pounds 1.13$ each. How much change will he receive from a $\pounds 20$ note?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Malcolm buys &#36;7&#36; packets of biscuits at &#36;73 \ \text{p}&#36; each and &#36;9&#36; large cakes at &#36;\pounds 1.13&#36; each. How much change will he receive from a &#36;\pounds 20&#36; note?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 7 \times 73 \ \text{p} + 9 \times \pounds 1.13 \\\\
                        &= 7 \times \pounds 0.73 + \pounds 10.17 \\\\
                        &= 5.11 + 10.17 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 15.28 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 20 - 15.28 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.72
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 4.72$

</div>
</div>
