<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What percentage is $15 \ \text{p}$ out of $\pounds 3.00?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What percentage is &#36;15 \ \text{p}&#36; out of &#36;\pounds 3.00?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Percentage}  &= \dfrac{15 \ \text{p}}{\pounds 3.00} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{15 \ \text{p}}{300 \ \text{p}} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {300} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 100} {300} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 100} {3 \times 100} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 5 \times \cancel {100}} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {100}} \\\\
                    &= 5 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Percentage}  &= \dfrac{15 \ \text{p}}{\pounds 3.00} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{15 \ \text{p}}{300 \ \text{p}} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {300} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 100} {300} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 100} {3 \times 100} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 5 \times \cancel {100}} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {100}} \\\\
                    &= 5 \%
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jack buys **four** apples.

He pays with a $\pounds 2$ coin.

He gets $\pounds 1.20$ change.

How much does **one** apple cost?

<img src="/assets/papers/woldingham-school/2014/sample-paper-1/questions/s02-q01-s01-i00.png" alt="papers/woldingham-school/2014/sample-paper-1/questions/s02-q01-s01-i00.png" title="papers/woldingham-school/2014/sample-paper-1/questions/s02-q01-s01-i00.png" data-slot="paper-image" data-kind="essential" data-size="normal" data-align="center" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 32rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jack buys **four** apples.

He pays with a &#36;\pounds 2&#36; coin.

He gets &#36;\pounds 1.20&#36; change.

How much does **one** apple cost?

&lt;PaperImage kind="essential" /&gt;

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of an apple}    &= \dfrac {2 - 1.20} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {0.80} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {0.20 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {0.20 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 0.20 \\\\
                            &= 20 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of an apple}    &= \dfrac {2 - 1.20} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {0.80} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {0.20 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {0.20 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 0.20 \\\\
                            &= 20 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;20 \ \text{p}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Oranges cost $15 \ \text{p}$ each. 

Raj has a $\pounds 1$ coin.

What is the greatest number of oranges Raj can buy with $\pounds 1$?

<img src="/assets/papers/woldingham-school/2014/sample-paper-1/questions/s02-q01-s02-i00.png" alt="papers/woldingham-school/2014/sample-paper-1/questions/s02-q01-s02-i00.png" title="papers/woldingham-school/2014/sample-paper-1/questions/s02-q01-s02-i00.png" data-slot="paper-image" data-kind="essential" data-size="normal" data-align="center" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 32rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Oranges cost &#36;15 \ \text{p}&#36; each. 

Raj has a &#36;\pounds 1&#36; coin.

What is the greatest number of oranges Raj can buy with &#36;\pounds 1&#36;?

&lt;PaperImage kind="essential" /&gt;

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Count}   &= \dfrac {\text{Total money}} {\text {Cost of an orange}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\pounds 1} {15 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {100 \ \text{p}} {15 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                &= 6 \ \text {remainder} \ 10 \\\\
                &\approx 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \ \text {oranges}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

                &= \dfrac {\pounds 1} {15 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {100 \ \text{p}} {15 \ \text{p}} \\\\
